    FROM `sku_product_variants` AS `s`
    WHERE `p0`.`product_id` = `s`.`product_id`) AS `ProductAmount`, (
    SELECT COALESCE(SUM(CAST(`s0`.`quantity` AS signed)), 0)
    FROM `sku_product_variants` AS `s0`
    WHERE `p0`.`product_id` = `s0`.`product_id`) AS `InStock`, (
    SELECT MAX(`s1`.`discount`)
    FROM `sku_product_variants` AS `s1`
    WHERE `p0`.`product_id` = `s1`.`product_id`) AS `Discount`, (
    SELECT COALESCE(SUM(`o`.`quantity`), 0)

    WHERE `o`.`product_id` = `p0`.`product_id`) AS `NoOfPurchased`, COALESCE((
    SELECT `p2`.`file_name`

SELECT `p0`.`product_id` AS `ProductId`, `p0`.`product_name` AS `ProductName`, `b0`.`business_category_name` AS `CategoryName`, `p1`.`prod_type_name` AS `ProductType`, `f`.`fabric_name` AS `Fabric`, `d`.`design_name` AS `Design`, `p0`.`supplier_name` AS `Supplier`, `p0`.`product_description` AS `ProductDescription`, (
    SELECT MIN(COALESCE(`s`.`discount_price`, `s`.`sku_cost`))
    FROM `sku_product_variants` AS `s`
    WHERE `p0`.`product_id` = `s`.`product_id`) AS `ProductAmount`, (
    SELECT COALESCE(SUM(CAST(`s0`.`quantity` AS signed)), 0)
    FROM `sku_product_variants` AS `s0`
    WHERE `p0`.`product_id` = `s0`.`product_id`) AS `InStock`, (
    SELECT MAX(`s1`.`discount`)
    FROM `sku_product_variants` AS `s1`
    WHERE `p0`.`product_id` = `s1`.`product_id`) AS `Discount`, (
    SELECT COALESCE(SUM(`o`.`quantity`), 0)
    FROM `orderdetails` AS `o`
    WHERE `o`.`product_id` = `p0`.`product_id`) AS `NoOfPurchased`, COALESCE((
    SELECT `p2`.`file_name`
    FROM `sku_product_variants` AS `s2`
    INNER JOIN `product_images` AS `p2` ON `s2`.`sku_id` = `p2`.`sku_id`
    WHERE `p0`.`product_id` = `s2`.`product_id`
    ORDER BY `p2`.`sort_order`
    LIMIT 1), (
    SELECT `p3`.`file_name`
    FROM `product_images` AS `p3`
    WHERE `p0`.`product_id` = `p3`.`product_id`
    ORDER BY `p3`.`sort_order`
    LIMIT 1)) AS `ProductImage`
FROM (
    SELECT `p`.`product_id`, `p`.`bus_reg_id`, `p`.`design_id`, `p`.`fabric_id`, `p`.`product_description`, `p`.`product_name`, `p`.`product_type_id`, `p`.`supplier_name`
    FROM `products` AS `p`
    WHERE `p`.`bus_reg_id` = @__storeId_0
    ORDER BY `p`.`product_id` DESC
    LIMIT @__p_2 OFFSET @__p_1
) AS `p0`
INNER JOIN `business_registers` AS `b` ON `p0`.`bus_reg_id` = `b`.`bus_reg_id`
INNER JOIN `business_categories` AS `b0` ON `b`.`bus_cat_id` = `b0`.`bus_cat_id`
LEFT JOIN `product_type` AS `p1` ON `p0`.`product_type_id` = `p1`.`prod_type_id`
LEFT JOIN `fabrics` AS `f` ON `p0`.`fabric_id` = `f`.`fabric_id`
LEFT JOIN `designs` AS `d` ON `p0`.`design_id` = `d`.`design_id`
ORDER BY `p0`.`product_id` DESC

    SELECT `p3`.`file_name`
    FROM `orders` AS `o1`
    INNER JOIN `payments` AS `p1` ON `o1`.`order_id` = `p1`.`order_id`
    INNER JOIN `orderdetails` AS `o2` ON `o1`.`order_id` = `o2`.`order_id`
    INNER JOIN `products` AS `p2` ON `o2`.`product_id` = `p2`.`product_id`
    INNER JOIN `sku_product_variants` AS `s0` ON `o2`.`sku_id` = `s0`.`sku_id`
    INNER JOIN `business_registers` AS `b0` ON `o2`.`store_id` = `b0`.`bus_reg_id`
    INNER JOIN `product_images` AS `p3` ON `s0`.`sku_id` = `p3`.`sku_id`
    WHERE ((((`o1`.`shopper_reg_id` = @__shopperRegId_0) AND (`p1`.`payment_status` = 'Paid')) AND (`p2`.`product_status` = 'Approved')) AND `p2`.`is_active`) AND ((((((`o0`.`product_id` = `o2`.`product_id`) AND (`o0`.`sku_id` = `o2`.`sku_id`)) AND (`o0`.`store_id` = `o2`.`store_id`)) AND (`p0`.`product_name` = `p2`.`product_name`)) AND ((`p0`.`product_image` = `p2`.`product_image`) OR (`p0`.`product_image` IS NULL AND (`p2`.`product_image` IS NULL)))) AND (`b`.`business_name` = `b0`.`business_name`))
    ORDER BY `p3`.`sort_order`, `p3`.`image_id`
    LIMIT 1), `p0`.`product_image`) AS `VariantImage`, `o0`.`store_id` AS `StoreId`, `b`.`business_name` AS `StoreName`, MAX(`o`.`order_date`) AS `LastOrderedOn`, (

    FROM `orders` AS `o3`
    INNER JOIN `payments` AS `p4` ON `o3`.`order_id` = `p4`.`order_id`
    INNER JOIN `orderdetails` AS `o4` ON `o3`.`order_id` = `o4`.`order_id`
    INNER JOIN `products` AS `p5` ON `o4`.`product_id` = `p5`.`product_id`
    INNER JOIN `sku_product_variants` AS `s1` ON `o4`.`sku_id` = `s1`.`sku_id`
    INNER JOIN `business_registers` AS `b1` ON `o4`.`store_id` = `b1`.`bus_reg_id`
    WHERE ((((`o3`.`shopper_reg_id` = @__shopperRegId_0) AND (`p4`.`payment_status` = 'Paid')) AND (`p5`.`product_status` = 'Approved')) AND `p5`.`is_active`) AND ((((((`o0`.`product_id` = `o4`.`product_id`) AND (`o0`.`sku_id` = `o4`.`sku_id`)) AND (`o0`.`store_id` = `o4`.`store_id`)) AND (`p0`.`product_name` = `p5`.`product_name`)) AND ((`p0`.`product_image` = `p5`.`product_image`) OR (`p0`.`product_image` IS NULL AND (`p5`.`product_image` IS NULL)))) AND (`b`.`business_name` = `b1`.`business_name`))
    ORDER BY `o3`.`order_date` DESC

        SELECT 1
        FROM `shipping_details` AS `s0`
        INNER JOIN `orders` AS `o0` ON `s0`.`order_id` = `o0`.`order_id`
        WHERE ((`o0`.`shopper_reg_id` = @__shopperRegId_0) AND (`s`.`order_id` = `s0`.`order_id`)) AND ((`s0`.`shipping_status` <> 'Delivered') OR `s0`.`delivered_date` IS NULL)) AS `c`

    INNER JOIN `orders` AS `o` ON `s`.`order_id` = `o`.`order_id`

FROM (
    SELECT `s`.`order_id` AS `OrderId`, MAX(`s`.`delivered_date`) AS `DeliveredDate`, 'Delivered' AS `ShippingStatus`, NOT EXISTS (
        SELECT 1
        FROM `shipping_details` AS `s0`
        INNER JOIN `orders` AS `o0` ON `s0`.`order_id` = `o0`.`order_id`
        WHERE ((`o0`.`shopper_reg_id` = @__shopperRegId_0) AND (`s`.`order_id` = `s0`.`order_id`)) AND ((`s0`.`shipping_status` <> 'Delivered') OR `s0`.`delivered_date` IS NULL)) AS `c`

SELECT `p`.`product_id` AS `ProductId`, `p`.`product_name` AS `ProductName`, `o`.`quantity` AS `Quantity`, `o`.`price` AS `UnitPrice`, `o`.`price` * CAST(`o`.`quantity` AS decimal(65,30)) AS `Amount`, `s`.`weight` AS `Weight`, `s`.`length` AS `Length`, `s`.`width` AS `Width`, `s`.`height` AS `Height`, COALESCE(`p1`.`file_name`, `p3`.`file_name`) AS `ProductImage`
FROM `orderdetails` AS `o`
INNER JOIN `products` AS `p` ON `o`.`product_id` = `p`.`product_id`
LEFT JOIN `sku_product_variants` AS `s` ON `o`.`sku_id` = `s`.`sku_id`
LEFT JOIN (
    SELECT `p0`.`file_name`, `p0`.`sku_id`
    FROM `product_images` AS `p0`
    WHERE `p0`.`sort_order` = 1
) AS `p1` ON `s`.`sku_id` = `p1`.`sku_id`
LEFT JOIN (
    SELECT `p2`.`file_name`, `p2`.`product_id`
    FROM `product_images` AS `p2`
    WHERE (`p2`.`sort_order` = 1) AND `p2`.`sku_id` IS NULL

        FROM `shipping_details` AS `s0`

SELECT `w`.`wishlist_id` AS `WishlistId`, `p`.`product_id` AS `ProductId`, `p`.`product_name` AS `ProductName`, `w`.`sku_id` AS `SkuId`, `p`.`buscat_id` AS `Buscatid`, `p`.`prod_subcat_id` AS `prod_sub_catid`, COALESCE(`p1`.`file_name`, `p3`.`file_name`) AS `VariantImageUrl`, COALESCE(`s`.`discount_price`, `s`.`sku_cost`) AS `Price`, `b`.`bus_reg_id` AS `StoreId`, `b`.`business_name` AS `StoreName`, (`p`.`product_status` = 'Approved') AND `p`.`is_active` AS `IsProductAvailable`
FROM `wishlist` AS `w`
INNER JOIN `products` AS `p` ON `w`.`product_id` = `p`.`product_id`
INNER JOIN `business_registers` AS `b` ON `w`.`bus_reg_id` = `b`.`bus_reg_id`
INNER JOIN `sku_product_variants` AS `s` ON `w`.`sku_id` = `s`.`sku_id`
LEFT JOIN (
    SELECT `p0`.`file_name`, `p0`.`sku_id`
    FROM `product_images` AS `p0`
    WHERE `p0`.`sort_order` = 1
) AS `p1` ON `w`.`sku_id` = `p1`.`sku_id`
LEFT JOIN (
    SELECT `p2`.`file_name`, `p2`.`product_id`
    FROM `product_images` AS `p2`
    WHERE (`p2`.`sort_order` = 1) AND `p2`.`sku_id` IS NULL
) AS `p3` ON `p`.`product_id` = `p3`.`product_id`
WHERE `w`.`shopper_reg_id` = @__shopperId_0

LIMIT @__p_2 OFFSET @__p_1
